[USER]

[GAME] Welcome to Secret Mafia! You are Player 6.
Your role: Villager
Team: Village
Description: A regular villager. Your goal is to identify and eliminate all Mafia members through voting during the day.

Players: Player 0, Player 1, Player 2, Player 3, Player 4, Player 5, Player 6, Player 7

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

[GAME] Player 2 was killed during the night.
[GAME] Day breaks. Discuss for 3 rounds, then a vote will follow.
[Player 7] **Player 7 (Detective) - Village Team**  
**Day Phase - Round 1**

Alright, everyone, let's start discussing.  

First off, I want to share what I found during the night. I investigated **Player 2**, and I can confirm that **Player 2 was killed during the night**. That means someone from the Mafia acted, and we need to figure out who.  

Also, I investigated **Player 3**, and I can confirm that **Player 3 is not a Mafia member**. So we can rule them out as a suspect.  

Now, the question is: who is the Mafia? We know that one of us is the killer, and they’re still among us. Let’s look at the players who are still alive.  

We know Player 2 is dead, so they’re out of the game. That leaves Players 0, 1, 3, 4, 5, 6, and me (Player 7).  

I’m going to ask everyone: did anyone see or hear anything suspicious last night? Did anyone act strangely?  

Let’s go around the table and hear from each of you. Who wants to speak first?

[COMPLETION]
**Player 6 (Villager) - Village Team**  
**Day Phase - Round 1**

I think I saw something last night. I was in the kitchen when I heard a loud noise coming from the storage room. I peeked through the door and saw someone moving around, but I couldn’t see their face clearly. It was too dark. I didn’t want to get involved, so I just went back to my room. I don’t know who it was, but I’m sure it wasn’t Player 3 because I saw them earlier in the day talking to Player 1.  

Does anyone else have any information?

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.

Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).
